rating: 4
review_text: Cool little local spot to grab food and drinks where you can pull up in your boat (dock or right onto the sand).
There's a small beach and an extra bar area with live music.

I am not a local (yet 😉) and felt a little overdressed arriving with a collared polo, khaki shorts and tennis shoes. So feel free to come as you are with swim trunks and flip flops.

The food was good and the staff were friendly.

They probably need a little more help to keep things cleaned up and don't be surprised to see the staff smoking behind the bar...but don't let that deter you from enjoying the views.

--- entity_google_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
review_id: 9345
entity_slug: pirates-cove
google_review_name: places/ChIJKaRD5v2nkIgRB_1fB_a3zR0/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT25kWk5HWkNZVGxOUWtoNlNqUk5OM296UzFwR1NVRRAB
rating: 5
review_text: Second trip to Pirates Cove. Same as the first—still the same old waterfront dive bar that feels like it’s been frozen in time. If you’ve spent any time growing up around water with parents who just kind of let you roam free so they can drink in peace, you know exactly what I mean. Now that I’m an adult, I somehow find myself dragging my own kids to places like this, just so my wife and I can have a drink while the kids do their thing in what we’ll consider a “safe” environment.

The food… it’s fine. It’s always fine. They serve it with the kind of indifference you’d expect from a place like this. If you’re the type who needs your meal served with a side of “care,” this probably isn’t your spot. The burgers? Solid. You get it one of two ways: their way or plain. Honestly, just take it their way and save everyone the trouble. Fries are decent—fresh-cut potato wedges—and the onion rings are hot, just like you want them. Drinks? Cold. A little overpriced, but whatever, it’s a damn vacation on the coast. If you were looking for deals, maybe you should’ve stayed in Missouri.

Would’ve snapped a pic of the food, but I was too hungry. And, let’s be real, you already know what a burger looks like.

id: 73cc2958-4536-495f-9186-7c726f133f5e
entity_slug: pirates-cove
reviewer_name: Shawn Shepherd
rating: 4
body: Cool little local spot to grab food and drinks where you can pull up in your boat (dock or right onto the sand).
There's a small beach and an extra bar area with live music.

I am not a local (yet 😉) and felt a little overdressed arriving with a collared polo, khaki shorts and tennis shoes. So feel free to come as you are with swim trunks and flip flops.

The food was good and the staff were friendly.

They probably need a little more help to keep things cleaned up and don't be surprised to see the staff smoking behind the bar...but don't let that deter you from enjoying the views.

--- entity_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
id: 5b2333cb-48e7-4dee-a104-fc912d7dce29
entity_slug: pirates-cove
reviewer_name: Phil R
rating: 5
body: Second trip to Pirates Cove. Same as the first—still the same old waterfront dive bar that feels like it’s been frozen in time. If you’ve spent any time growing up around water with parents who just kind of let you roam free so they can drink in peace, you know exactly what I mean. Now that I’m an adult, I somehow find myself dragging my own kids to places like this, just so my wife and I can have a drink while the kids do their thing in what we’ll consider a “safe” environment.

The food… it’s fine. It’s always fine. They serve it with the kind of indifference you’d expect from a place like this. If you’re the type who needs your meal served with a side of “care,” this probably isn’t your spot. The burgers? Solid. You get it one of two ways: their way or plain. Honestly, just take it their way and save everyone the trouble. Fries are decent—fresh-cut potato wedges—and the onion rings are hot, just like you want them. Drinks? Cold. A little overpriced, but whatever, it’s a damn vacation on the coast. If you were looking for deals, maybe you should’ve stayed in Missouri.

Would’ve snapped a pic of the food, but I was too hungry. And, let’s be real, you already know what a burger looks like.
